## The ticket

Replace the ad-hoc rate-limit bookkeeping in the online request path with one capacity-budget module: a single place that decodes provider limit headers, and a tracker whose per-minute buckets are seeded, refilled and settled through an injectable clock.

### New module `src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/capacity_budget.py`

- Exports the constants `DEFAULT_MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE = 200`, `DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE_COMBINED = 100_000`, `DEFAULT_MAX_INPUT_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 100_000`, `DEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 40_000`, `LIMIT_ORIGIN_CONFIGURED = "configured"`, `LIMIT_ORIGIN_DEFAULTED = "defaulted"`, `LIMIT_ORIGIN_UNLIMITED = "unlimited"`.
- `TokenLimitStrategy` (the enum, moved here from `online_status_tracker`, members `combined`, `seperate`, `default`) is re-exported from `online_status_tracker` for existing importers.
- `class CapacityExceedsLimitError(ValueError)` with `__init__(self, axis: str, requested: int, limit: float)`, storing `.axis`, `.requested`, `.limit`.
- `@dataclass(frozen=True) class RateLimitReading` with fields, in order: `max_requests_per_minute: int | None`, `max_tokens_per_minute: int | _TokenUsage | None`, `token_limit_strategy: TokenLimitStrategy`, `source_headers: tuple[str, ...]`.
- The header-name tuples `REQUEST_LIMIT_HEADERS = ("x-ratelimit-limit-requests", "anthropic-ratelimit-requests-limit")`, `INPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = ("x-ratelimit-limit-input-tokens", "anthropic-ratelimit-input-tokens-limit")`, `OUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = ("x-ratelimit-limit-output-tokens", "anthropic-ratelimit-output-tokens-limit")`, `TOTAL_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = ("x-ratelimit-limit-tokens", "anthropic-ratelimit-tokens-limit")`.
- Stdlib only (`math`, `re`, `dataclasses`, `enum`, `typing`); no new dependency; Python 3.10 syntax without `from __future__ import annotations`.

### `def parse_limit_value(raw: object) -> int | None`

- The single value decoder. `bool` is rejected (`None`) even though it is an `int` subclass; an `int` returns itself when `> 0`, else `None`.
- A `str` is stripped and matched against `^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)([kKmM]?)$`; the numeric part is multiplied by `1` / `1_000` / `1_000_000` for suffix `""` / `k`,`K` / `m`,`M`, then `math.floor`ed; the result is returned when `> 0`, else `None`.
- Everything else — `float`, `None`, `bytes`, `"1,000"`, `"1e3"`, `"-5"`, `"unknown"`, `""` — is `None`. Nothing raises: a malformed header is absent, not fatal.
- Examples: `parse_limit_value("1.5k") == 1500`, `parse_limit_value("2M") == 2000000`, `parse_limit_value("40000.5") == 40000`, `parse_limit_value("0") is None`.

### `def read_rate_limit_headers(headers: t.Mapping[str, object]) -> RateLimitReading`

- Module-level, mutates nothing. Lower-cases the incoming keys once, then consults only the four name tuples above, in tuple order, first present-and-parseable name winning.
- No `*-remaining`, `*-reset` or `llm_provider-*` key is ever read, and there are no hardcoded numeric fallbacks anywhere in the reading — substituting defaults is the tracker's job.
- `anthropic-ratelimit-input-tokens-limit` feeds the **input** axis and `anthropic-ratelimit-output-tokens-limit` the **output** axis.
- Strategy selection lives in the reading: when both an input and an output limit parse to positive ints the result is `seperate` with `_TokenUsage(input=…, output=…)`, and any total-token header present is ignored; otherwise the result is `combined` using the total-token header, and a lone half of the pair is discarded. Nothing usable gives `max_tokens_per_minute=None`, `combined`.
- `source_headers` lists the exact header names consumed, request axis first, then `(input, output)` or `(total,)`; `()` when nothing was consumed.

### `OnlineStatusTracker` fields

- `max_tokens_per_minute: int | _TokenUsage | None = 0` is declared exactly once (the duplicate at `online_status_tracker.py:64` goes away); `max_requests_per_minute: int | None = 0`; `available_request_capacity: float | None`; `available_token_capacity: float | _TokenUsage | None`.
- New `capacity_clock: t.Callable[[], float] = field(default=time.time, repr=False, compare=False)`. Every wall-clock read in `update_capacity` goes through `self.capacity_clock()`, and `__post_init__` re-seeds `self.last_update_time = self.capacity_clock()`. `start_time`, `_last_stats_update`, the display code and `cool_down_if_rate_limit_error` keep calling `time.time()` directly.
- New `token_limit_origin: str` and `request_limit_origin: str`, both `field(default=LIMIT_ORIGIN_CONFIGURED, init=False)`.

### `OnlineStatusTracker.__post_init__` normalisation

- Shape coercion: under `combined` a `_TokenUsage` limit collapses to its `.total`; under `seperate` an `int` limit `N` becomes `_TokenUsage(input=N, output=N)`.
- A limit of `None` means **unlimited**: the limit stays `None`, the matching available capacity is `None`, the origin is `"unlimited"`. Under `seperate` this is expressed per axis as `_TokenUsage(input=None, output=None)`, and a whole-token-limit `None` normalises to exactly that object.
- A limit of `0` (or, under `seperate`, an axis that is `0`) means **nobody told us**: it is replaced by the matching `DEFAULT_*` constant axis by axis, and the origin becomes `"defaulted"` if any axis was replaced. A positive number gives origin `"configured"`.
- Both buckets are then seeded to their full effective per-minute limit — `available_request_capacity = float(max_requests_per_minute)`, and `float(max_tokens_per_minute)` or `_TokenUsage(input=…, output=…)` for tokens — overwriting whatever the caller passed for either available-capacity field.

### `OnlineStatusTracker` capacity operations

- `def update_capacity(self) -> None`: `elapsed = now - self.last_update_time` is clamped with `elapsed = max(0.0, elapsed)` before refilling, refill is capped above at the limit, and `self.last_update_time = now` is assigned unconditionally, including when `now` is earlier than the previous reading. Under `seperate` the refill increment is `math.floor(limit_axis * elapsed / 60.0)`, keeping axis values `int`.
- `def has_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage) -> bool`, `def consume_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage) -> None`, `def free_capacity(self, used: _TokenUsage, blocked: _TokenUsage) -> None`. `free_capacity` settles the token axes by `blocked - used` (per axis under `seperate`, `blocked.total - used.total` under `combined`).
- Under `seperate`, every mutation of `available_token_capacity` rebinds a freshly constructed `_TokenUsage(input=…, output=…)` instead of assigning to `.input`/`.output`, so `.total` is never stale.
- An axis whose capacity is `None` is read, skipped and left `None` by all of these; an unlimited tracker admits any estimate.

### `CapacityExceedsLimitError`

- `has_capacity` first asks whether the estimate could ever be satisfied: for each axis with a non-`None` limit, if the requested amount is strictly greater than the per-minute limit it raises `CapacityExceedsLimitError(axis, requested, limit)` instead of returning `False`.
- The check runs **before** `update_capacity()`, so a raise leaves `last_update_time` and both buckets untouched. Axis names and order: `"total"` under `combined`; `"input"` then `"output"` under `seperate`. An unlimited axis never raises, and a merely-empty bucket still returns `False`.
- `str(exc) == f"request needs {requested} {axis} capacity but the per-minute limit is {int(limit)}"`.

### `BaseOnlineRequestProcessor`

- `def _reserve_capacity(self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, messages: list) -> _TokenUsage | None`: a plain synchronous method that calls `self.estimate_total_tokens(messages)` exactly once and unconditionally — including when `status_tracker.max_tokens_per_minute is None` — then `status_tracker.has_capacity(estimate)` (letting `CapacityExceedsLimitError` propagate); on `True` it consumes and returns the very `_TokenUsage` object it estimated, on `False` it returns `None` having consumed nothing, neither tokens nor the request slot.
- Both reservation loops (lines 384-391 and 432-439) become `while (token_estimate := self._reserve_capacity(status_tracker, request.generic_request.messages)) is None: await asyncio.sleep(0.1)`, so the sleep remains the only await.
- `def _free_capacity(self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, used_capacity: _TokenUsage, blocked_capacity: _TokenUsage) -> None` delegates to the tracker.
- In `handle_single_request_with_retries` the reservation is released exactly once per attempt on **every** terminal path — the success path, the requeued-failure path and the exhausted-failure path — rather than only at line 584. The `try/except/else/finally` structure, `config.invalid_finish_reasons`, `update_stats`, `update_cost_projection` and `append_generic_response` are otherwise unchanged.
- The dead `def free_capacity(self, tracker, tokens)` at line 312 (empty body, no caller) is deleted: `hasattr(BaseOnlineRequestProcessor, "free_capacity") is False`.
- `def apply_rate_limit_reading(self, reading: RateLimitReading) -> None` is the only place that writes processor state from a reading: it sets `self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute`, `self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute` and `self.token_limit_strategy = reading.token_limit_strategy`, then re-reads `self.default_max_tokens_per_minute` from `_DEFAULT_COST_MAP["online"]["default"]["ratelimit"]["max_tokens_per_minute"]` for the *new* strategy (`int` for `combined`, `_TokenUsage(**block)` for `seperate`), fixing the case where the anthropic subclass switches to `seperate` after `__init__` already picked the `combined` block.

### Provider processors

- `anthropic_online_request_processor.get_header_based_rate_limits()` and `litellm_online_request_processor.get_header_based_rate_limits()` delegate to `read_rate_limit_headers()`, dropping the swapped anthropic mapping, the `4000`/`80000`/`400000` fallbacks, the `-remaining` reads and the in-place `self.token_limit_strategy = …` mutation.
- `openai_online_request_processor.get_header_based_rate_limits()` delegates for the header half only (lines 150-159); the `RATE_LIMIT_HEADER` provider table and its `rps`/`tps` scaling stay exactly as they are.
- `BaseOnlineRequestProcessor.max_requests_per_minute` / `.max_tokens_per_minute` / `.max_concurrent_requests` keep their manual → header → default precedence.

### Out of scope

`cool_down_if_rate_limit_error`, `config.seconds_to_pause_on_rate_limit`, `config.max_retries`, the retry queue and its ordering, `num_rate_limit_errors`, `time_of_last_rate_limit_error`, and `_TokenUsage` itself are not touched.

### Tests

No network, no real `asyncio.sleep` waits: construct `OnlineStatusTracker` directly with an injected clock, and drive a stub subclass of `BaseOnlineRequestProcessor` built from `OnlineRequestProcessorConfig(model="gpt-4o-mini")` implementing the five abstract methods. `pytest` + `pytest-asyncio` for the one coroutine.

## Requirements settled earlier

These were agreed before the ticket was written. They are not optional.

### r1

- **rule** — Token capacity may go negative, but each axis is floored at `-CAPACITY_DEBT_FLOOR_FRACTION * limit` with `CAPACITY_DEBT_FLOOR_FRACTION: float = 0.25` exported from the capacity-budget module. The floor is `-limit * 0.25` as a `float` for the `combined` axis. Worked case: a tracker with `max_tokens_per_minute=1_000`, after `consume_capacity(_TokenUsage(input=700, output=100))` and then settling `used=_TokenUsage(input=1000, output=500)` against `blocked=_TokenUsage(input=700, output=100)`, ends at `available_token_capacity == -250.0`.
- **scope** — A tracker constructed with `max_tokens_per_minute=0` (defaulted to 100_000) floors at `-25000.0`, and under `seperate` each axis floors against its own limit: with `_TokenUsage(input=1_000, output=500)` an over-settled tracker lands on `_TokenUsage(input=-250, output=-125, total=-375)`. An axis whose limit and capacity are `None` has no floor and stays `None`. `available_request_capacity` is never floored.
- **exclusions_or_crossover** — The refill in `update_capacity` capping an axis at its limit, and a release capping an axis at its limit, leave the clamp counter untouched. Only the lower debt floor counts — a settlement whose result stays above the floor (e.g. `available_token_capacity == 100.0` on a 1_000-token tracker) leaves the counter at `0`.
- **observability** — A tracker field `num_capacity_debt_clamps: int = 0` increments by exactly one per call that clamped at least one axis.

### r2

- **rule** — `free_capacity(used, blocked)`: the token axes gain `blocked - used` and `available_request_capacity` is left exactly as `consume_capacity` left it. `refund_capacity(blocked: _TokenUsage) -> None`: the token axes gain the whole `blocked` estimate back (capped above at the limit) and `available_request_capacity` gains exactly `1.0`, capped above at `max_requests_per_minute`. The processor's `_refund_capacity(self, status_tracker, blocked_capacity)` delegates to `status_tracker.refund_capacity(blocked_capacity)`.
- **scope** — Every terminal path through the `except Exception` branch of `handle_single_request_with_retries` calls the refund — both the requeued case (`attempts_left > 0`) and the exhausted case (`attempts_left == 0`). On a tracker with `max_requests_per_minute=60`, `max_tokens_per_minute=10_000` and a reservation of `_TokenUsage(input=700, output=300)`, either failure path ends at `available_request_capacity == 60.0` and `available_token_capacity == 10000.0`, while the success path with reported usage `_TokenUsage(input=700, output=100)` ends at `59.0` / `9200.0`.
- **exclusions_or_crossover** — The failure refund uses the full blocked estimate and ignores `generic_response.token_usage` — e.g. a response with `finish_reason="length"` (in `config.invalid_finish_reasons`) reporting `_TokenUsage(input=900, output=800)` against a blocked estimate of `_TokenUsage(input=900, output=100)` returns the estimate.
- **observability** — Two tracker counters, `num_capacity_settlements: int = 0` and `num_capacity_refunds: int = 0`, each incremented by exactly one by its own operation and by neither the other's. Both counters still increment when the affected capacity is `None` and the operation is a no-op on every axis.

console=self._console,\\n            refresh_per_second=4,\\n            transient=True,\\n        )\\n        self._live.start()\\n\\n    def _start_tqdm_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Start the tqdm progress tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self.pbar = tqdm.tqdm(\\n            total=self.total_requests,\\n            initial=self.num_tasks_already_completed,\\n            desc=f\\"Processing {self.model}\\",\\n            unit=\\"req\\",\\n        )\\n        # Initialize last update time for periodic updates\\n        self._last_stats_update = time.time()\\n        self._last_display_update = time.time()\\n        # Log initial stats\\n        self._log_stats()\\n\\n    def _update_tqdm_description(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the tqdm description.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if not USE_RICH_DISPLAY and self.pbar:\\n            self.pbar.set_description(\\n                f\\"Processing {MODEL}{self.model}{END} \\"\\n                f\\"[{SUCCESS}{self.num_tasks_succeeded} succeeded{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"{WARNING}{self.num_tasks_in_progress} in progress{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"{ERROR}{self.num_tasks_failed} failed{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"{COST}${self.total_cost:.3f} spent{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"{METRIC}{self.num_tasks_started / max(0.001, (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60):.1f} req/min{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"{METRIC}{self.num_tasks_succeeded / max(0.001, (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60):.1f} res/min{END}]\\"\\n            )\\n\\n    def _log_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Log current statistics when using tqdm mode.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if not USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n            input_tpm = self.total_prompt_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n            output_tpm = self.total_completion_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n            req_per_min = self.num_tasks_started / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n            res_per_min = self.num_tasks_succeeded / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n            # Calculate averages based on completed tasks or in-progress tasks\\n            total_tasks = max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded + self.num_tasks_in_progress)\\n            avg_prompt = self.total_prompt_tokens / total_tasks\\n            avg_completion = self.total_completion_tokens / total_tasks\\n            projected_total = self.total_cost + self.projected_remaining_cost\\n            cost_per_minute = self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n            # Update TQDM description\\n            self._update_tqdm_description()\\n\\n            # Add curator viewer link if available\\n            viewer_msg = \\"\\"\\n            if self.viewer_client and self.viewer_client.hosted and self.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url:\\n                viewer_msg = f\\"\\\\n{HEADER}Curator Viewer:{END} {self.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url}\\"\\n            else:\\n                viewer_msg = f\\"\\\\n{HEADER}Curator Viewer:{END} Disabled (Set CURATOR_VIEWER=1 to view at {PUBLIC_CURATOR_VIEWER_HOME_URL})\\"\\n\\n            stats_msg = (\\n                f\\"{viewer_msg}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Requests:{END} Total: {METRIC}{self.total_requests}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Cached: {SUCCESS}{self.num_tasks_already_completed}✓{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Success: {SUCCESS}{self.num_tasks_succeeded}✓{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Failed: {ERROR}{self.num_tasks_failed}✗{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"In Progress: {WARNING}{self.num_tasks_in_progress}⋯{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Req/min: {METRIC}{req_per_min:.1f}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Res/min: {METRIC}{res_per_min:.1f}{END}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Tokens:{END} Avg Input: {METRIC}{avg_prompt:.0f}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Input TPM: {METRIC}{input_tpm:.0f}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Avg Output: {METRIC}{avg_completion:.0f}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Output TPM: {METRIC}{output_tpm:.0f}{END}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Cost:{END} Current: {COST}${self.total_cost:.3f}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Est. Total: {COST}${projected_total:.3f}{END} {DIM}(${self.projected_remaining_cost:.3f} remaining){DIM}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Rate: {COST}${cost_per_minute:.3f}/min{END}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Rate Limits:{END} RPM: {METRIC}{self.max_requests_per_minute}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"TPM: {METRIC}{self.max_tokens_per_minute}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"TPM Strategy: {METRIC}{self.token_limit_strategy} token limit{END}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Model:{END} Name: {MODEL}{self.model}{END}\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"{HEADER}Model Pricing:{END} Per 1M tokens: \\"\\n                f\\"Input: {COST}{self.input_cost_str}{END} • \\"\\n                f\\"Output: {COST}{self.output_cost_str}{END}\\"\\n            )\\n            logger.info(stats_msg)\\n\\n    def update_display(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update the display based on current mode.\\"\\"\\"\\n        current_time = time.time()\\n\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._refresh_console()\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                # Always update progress bar position\\n                self.pbar.n = self.num_tasks_succeeded + self.num_tasks_already_completed\\n                self.pbar.refresh()\\n                self._update_tqdm_description()\\n\\n                # Update stats in any of these conditions:\\n                # 1. When tasks change state (in_progress, succeeded, failed)\\n                # 2. Every _STATUS_UPDATE_INTERVAL seconds\\n                # 3. When costs or tokens change\\n                should_update = (\\n                    current_time - self._last_stats_update >= _STATUS_UPDATE_INTERVAL  # Time-based update\\n                    and (\\n                        self.num_tasks_in_progress != getattr(self, \\"_last_in_progress\\", -1)  # Task state change\\n                        or self.num_tasks_succeeded != getattr(self, \\"_last_succeeded\\", -1)\\n                        or self.num_tasks_failed != getattr(self, \\"_last_failed\\", -1)\\n                        or self.total_tokens != getattr(self, \\"_last_total_tokens\\", -1)  # Token/cost changes\\n                        or self.total_cost != getattr(self, \\"_last_total_cost\\", -1)\\n                    )\\n                )\\n\\n                if should_update:\\n                    self._log_stats()\\n                    self._last_stats_update = current_time\\n                    # Store current values for next comparison\\n                    self._last_in_progress = self.num_tasks_in_progress\\n                    self._last_succeeded = self.num_tasks_succeeded\\n                    self._last_failed = self.num_tasks_failed\\n                    self._last_total_tokens = self.total_tokens\\n                    self._last_total_cost = self.total_cost\\n\\n    def _refresh_console(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Refresh the console display with latest stats.\\"\\"\\"\\n        # Calculate stats\\n        elapsed_minutes = (time.time() - self.start_time) / 60\\n        req_per_min = self.num_tasks_started / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        res_per_min = self.num_tasks_succeeded / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        input_tpm = self.total_prompt_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_completion_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        avg_prompt = self.total_prompt_tokens / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded)\\n        avg_completion = self.total_completion_tokens / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded)\\n\\n        # Calculate projected total\\n        projected_total = self.total_cost + self.projected_remaining_cost\\n\\n        # Update max concurrent requests seen\\n        self.max_concurrent_requests_seen = max(self.max_concurrent_requests_seen, self.num_tasks_in_progress)\\n\\n        # Format stats text\\n        stats_text = (\\n            f\\"[bold white]Requests:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Total:[/white] [blue]{self.total_requests}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Cached:[/white] [green]{self.num_tasks_already_completed}✓[/green] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Success:[/white] [green]{self.num_tasks_succeeded}✓[/green] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Failed:[/white] [red]{self.num_tasks_failed}✗[/red] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]In Progress:[/white] [yellow]{self.num_tasks_in_progress}⋯[/yellow] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Req/min:[/white] [blue]{req_per_min:.1f}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Res/min:[/white] [blue]{res_per_min:.1f}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Tokens:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Avg Input:[/white] [blue]{avg_prompt:.0f}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input TPM:[/white] [blue]{input_tpm:.0f}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Avg Output:[/white] [blue]{avg_completion:.0f}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output TPM:[/white] [blue]{output_tpm:.0f}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Cost:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Current:[/white] [magenta]${self.total_cost:.3f}[/magenta] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Est. Total:[/white] [magenta]${projected_total:.3f}[/magenta] [dim](${self.projected_remaining_cost:.3f} remaining)[/dim] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Rate:[/white] [magenta]${self.total_cost / max(0.01, elapsed_minutes):.3f}/min[/magenta]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Rate Limits:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]RPM:[/white] [blue]{self.max_requests_per_minute}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]TPM:[/white] [blue]{self.max_tokens_per_minute}[/blue] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]TPM Strategy:[/white] [blue]{self.token_limit_strategy} token limit[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Model:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Name:[/white] [blue]{self.model}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            f\\"[bold white]Model Pricing:[/bold white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Per 1M tokens:[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Input:[/white] {self.input_cost_str} \\"\\n            f\\"[white]•[/white] \\"\\n            f\\"[white]Output:[/white] {self.output_cost_str}\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        # Add curator viewer link if client is available and hosted\\n        if self.viewer_client and self.viewer_client.hosted and self.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url:\\n            viewer_text = (\\n                f\\"[bold white]Curator Viewer:[/bold white] \\"\\n                f\\"[blue][link={self.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url}]:sparkles: Open Curator Viewer[/link] :sparkles:[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n                f\\"[dim]{self.viewer_client.curator_viewer_url}[/dim]\\\\n\\"\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            viewer_text = (\\n                \\"[bold white]Curator Viewer:[/bold white] [yellow]Disabled[/yellow]\\\\n\\"\\n                \\"Set [yellow]CURATOR_VIEWER=[cyan]1[/cyan][/yellow] to view your data live at \\"\\n                f\\"[blue]{PUBLIC_CURATOR_VIEWER_HOME_URL}[/blue]\\\\n\\"\\n            )\\n        stats_text = viewer_text + stats_text\\n\\n        # Update main progress bar\\n        self._progress.update(\\n            self._task_id,\\n            completed=self.num_tasks_succeeded + self.num_tasks_already_completed,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Update stats display\\n        self._stats.update(\\n            self._stats_task_id,\\n            description=stats_text,\\n        )\\n\\n    def update_stats(self, token_usage: _TokenUsage, cost: float):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update statistics in the tracker with token usage and cost.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if token_usage:\\n            self.total_prompt_tokens += token_usage.input\\n            self.total_completion_tokens += token_usage.output\\n            self.total_tokens += token_usage.total\\n        if cost:\\n            self.total_cost += cost\\n\\n        self.update_display()\\n\\n    def stop_tracker(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Stop the tracker.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n                # Stop the live display\\n                self._live.stop()\\n                # Print the final progress state\\n                self._console.print(self._progress)\\n                self._console.print(self._stats)\\n        else:\\n            if self.pbar:\\n                self.pbar.close()\\n                # Log final stats\\n                self._log_stats()\\n\\n        # Display final statistics table\\n        self.display_final_stats()\\n\\n        # Clean up non-serializable fields before telemetry\\n        self.viewer_client = None\\n        temp_pbar = self.pbar\\n        self.pbar = None\\n        metadata = asdict(self)\\n        metadata.pop(\\"viewer_client\\", None)\\n        metadata.pop(\\"pbar\\", None)\\n        # Restore pbar if needed\\n        self.pbar = temp_pbar\\n\\n        telemetry_client.capture(\\n            TelemetryEvent(\\n                event_type=\\"OnlineRequest\\",\\n                metadata=metadata,\\n            )\\n        )\\n\\n    def display_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if USE_RICH_DISPLAY:\\n            self._display_rich_final_stats()\\n        else:\\n            self._display_simple_final_stats()\\n\\n    def _display_rich_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics using rich table.\\"\\"\\"\\n        table = Table(title=\\"Final Curator Statistics\\", box=box.ROUNDED)\\n        table.add_column(\\"Section/Metric\\", style=\\"cyan\\")\\n        table.add_column(\\"Value\\", style=\\"yellow\\")\\n\\n        # Model Information\\n        table.add_row(\\"Model\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Name\\", f\\"[blue]{self.model}[/blue]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Rate Limit (RPM)\\", f\\"[blue]{self.max_requests_per_minute}[/blue]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Rate Limit (TPM)\\", f\\"[blue]{self.max_tokens_per_minute}[/blue]\\")\\n\\n        # Request Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"Requests\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Processed\\", str(self.num_tasks_succeeded + self.num_tasks_failed))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Successful\\", f\\"[green]{self.num_tasks_succeeded}[/green]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Failed\\", f\\"[red]{self.num_tasks_failed}[/red]\\")\\n\\n        # Token Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"Tokens\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Tokens Used\\", f\\"{self.total_tokens:,}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Input Tokens\\", f\\"{self.total_prompt_tokens:,}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Output Tokens\\", f\\"{self.total_completion_tokens:,}\\")\\n        if self.num_tasks_succeeded > 0:\\n            table.add_row(\\n                \\"Average Tokens per Request\\",\\n                f\\"{int(self.total_tokens / self.num_tasks_succeeded)}\\",\\n            )\\n            table.add_row(\\n                \\"Average Input Tokens\\",\\n                f\\"{int(self.total_prompt_tokens / self.num_tasks_succeeded)}\\",\\n            )\\n            table.add_row(\\n                \\"Average Output Tokens\\",\\n                f\\"{int(self.total_completion_tokens / self.num_tasks_succeeded)}\\",\\n            )\\n        # Cost Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"Costs\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Cost\\", f\\"[red]${self.total_cost:.3f}[/red]\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\n            \\"Average Cost per Request\\",\\n            f\\"[red]${self.total_cost / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded):.3f}[/red]\\",\\n        )\\n        table.add_row(\\"Input Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.input_cost_str)\\n        table.add_row(\\"Output Cost per 1M Tokens\\", self.output_cost_str)\\n\\n        # Performance Statistics\\n        table.add_row(\\"Performance\\", \\"\\", style=\\"bold magenta\\")\\n        elapsed_time = time.time() - self.start_time\\n        elapsed_minutes = elapsed_time / 60\\n        req_per_min = self.num_tasks_started / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        res_per_min = self.num_tasks_succeeded / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        input_tpm = self.total_prompt_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_completion_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n        table.add_row(\\"Total Time\\", f\\"{elapsed_time:.2f}s\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\n            \\"Average Time per Request\\",\\n            f\\"{elapsed_time / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded):.2f}s\\",\\n        )\\n        table.add_row(\\"Requests per Minute\\", f\\"{req_per_min:.1f}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Responses per Minute\\", f\\"{res_per_min:.1f}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Max Concurrent Requests\\", str(self.max_concurrent_requests_seen))\\n        table.add_row(\\"Input Tokens per Minute\\", f\\"{input_tpm:.1f}\\")\\n        table.add_row(\\"Output Tokens per Minute\\", f\\"{output_tpm:.1f}\\")\\n\\n        self._console.print(table)\\n\\n    def _display_simple_final_stats(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Display final statistics in plain text format.\\"\\"\\"\\n        elapsed_time = time.time() - self.start_time\\n        elapsed_minutes = elapsed_time / 60\\n        rpm = self.num_tasks_succeeded / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        input_tpm = self.total_prompt_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n        output_tpm = self.total_completion_tokens / max(0.001, elapsed_minutes)\\n\\n        stats = [\\n            f\\"\\\\n{HEADER}Final Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Model Information:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Model: {MODEL}{self.model}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Rate Limit (RPM): {METRIC}{self.max_requests_per_minute}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Rate Limit (TPM): {METRIC}{self.max_tokens_per_minute}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Request Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Requests: {METRIC}{self.total_requests}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Cached: {SUCCESS}{self.num_tasks_already_completed}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Successful: {SUCCESS}{self.num_tasks_succeeded}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Failed: {ERROR}{self.num_tasks_failed}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Token Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Tokens Used: {METRIC}{self.total_tokens:,}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Input Tokens: {METRIC}{self.total_prompt_tokens:,}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Output Tokens: {METRIC}{self.total_completion_tokens:,}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Tokens per Request: {METRIC}{int(self.total_tokens / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded))}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Input Tokens: {METRIC}{int(self.total_prompt_tokens / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded))}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Output Tokens: {METRIC}{int(self.total_completion_tokens / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded))}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Cost Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Cost: {COST}${self.total_cost:.3f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Cost per Request: {COST}${self.total_cost / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded):.3f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Input Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.input_cost_str}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Output Cost per 1M Tokens: {COST}{self.output_cost_str}{END}\\",\\n            \\"\\",\\n            f\\"{HEADER}Performance Statistics:{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Total Time: {METRIC}{elapsed_time:.2f}s{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Average Time per Request: {METRIC}{elapsed_time / max(1, self.num_tasks_succeeded):.2f}s{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Requests per Minute: {METRIC}{rpm:.1f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Max Concurrent Requests: {METRIC}{self.max_concurrent_requests_seen}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Input Tokens per Minute: {METRIC}{input_tpm:.1f}{END}\\",\\n            f\\"  Output Tokens per Minute: {METRIC}{output_tpm:.1f}{END}\\",\\n        ]\\n        logger.info(\\"\\\\n\\".join(stats))\\n\\n    def update_capacity(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update available capacity based on time elapsed.\\"\\"\\"\\n        current_time = time.time()\\n        seconds_since_update = current_time - self.last_update_time\\n        if self.max_requests_per_minute is not None:\\n            self.available_request_capacity = min(\\n                self.available_request_capacity + self.max_requests_per_minute * seconds_since_update / 60.0,\\n                self.max_requests_per_minute,\\n            )\\n\\n        if self.token_limit_strategy == TokenLimitStrategy.combined:\\n            if self.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n                self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(int, self.available_token_capacity)\\n                self.max_tokens_per_minute = t.cast(int, self.max_tokens_per_minute)\\n                self.available_token_capacity = min(\\n                    self.available_token_capacity + self.max_tokens_per_minute * seconds_since_update / 60.0,\\n                    self.max_tokens_per_minute,\\n                )\\n        else:\\n            self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(_TokenUsage, self.available_token_capacity)\\n            self.max_tokens_per_minute = t.cast(_TokenUsage, self.max_tokens_per_minute)\\n            if self.max_tokens_per_minute.input is not None:\\n                self.available_token_capacity.input = min(\\n                    self.available_token_capacity.input + self.max_tokens_per_minute.input * seconds_since_update / 60.0,\\n                    self.max_tokens_per_minute.input,\\n                )\\n            if self.max_tokens_per_minute.output is not None:\\n                self.available_token_capacity.output = min(\\n                    self.available_token_capacity.output + self.max_tokens_per_minute.output * seconds_since_update / 60.0,\\n                    self.max_tokens_per_minute.output,\\n                )\\n\\n        self.last_update_time = current_time\\n\\n    def has_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage) -> bool:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if there\'s enough capacity for a request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        self.update_capacity()\\n        if self.token_limit_strategy == TokenLimitStrategy.combined:\\n            has_capacity = self._check_combined_capacity(token_estimate)\\n        else:\\n            has_capacity = self._check_seperate_capacity(token_estimate)\\n\\n        # This is a very loud log, enable only when needed\\n        # if not has_capacity:\\n        #     logger.debug(\\n        #         f\\"No capacity for request with {token_estimate} tokens.\\"\\n        #         f\\"Available capacity: {self.available_token_capacity} tokens, \\"\\n        #         f\\"{int(self.available_request_capacity)} requests.\\"\\n        #     )\\n        return has_capacity\\n\\n    def _check_combined_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage):\\n        self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(int, self.available_token_capacity)\\n        if self.max_requests_per_minute is None and self.max_tokens_per_minute is None:\\n            return True\\n\\n        token_estimate = token_estimate.total\\n        has_capacity = self.available_request_capacity >= 1 and self.available_token_capacity >= token_estimate\\n        return has_capacity\\n\\n    def _check_seperate_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage):\\n        self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(_TokenUsage, self.available_token_capacity)\\n        if self.max_tokens_per_minute.total is None and self.max_requests_per_minute is None:\\n            return True\\n\\n        has_capacity = (\\n            self.available_request_capacity >= 1\\n            and self.available_token_capacity.input >= token_estimate.input\\n            and self.available_token_capacity.output >= token_estimate.output\\n        )\\n        return has_capacity\\n\\n    def consume_capacity(self, token_estimate: _TokenUsage):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Consume capacity for a request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.max_requests_per_minute is not None:\\n            self.available_request_capacity -= 1\\n        if self.token_limit_strategy == TokenLimitStrategy.combined:\\n            if self.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n                self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(float, self.available_token_capacity)\\n                self.available_token_capacity -= token_estimate.total\\n        else:\\n            self.available_token_capacity = t.cast(_TokenUsage, self.available_token_capacity)\\n\\n            if self.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n                self.available_token_capacity.input -= token_estimate.input\\n                self.available_token_capacity.output -= token_estimate.output\\n\\n    def free_capacity(self, used: _TokenUsage, blocked: _TokenUsage):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Free extra consumed capacity.\\n\\n        Note: This can be a negative number\\n        incase of under estimation of consumed capacity.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.token_limit_strategy == TokenLimitStrategy.seperate:\\n            input_free = blocked.input - used.input\\n            output_free = blocked.output - used.output\\n            self.available_token_capacity.input += input_free\\n            self.available_token_capacity.output += output_free\\n        else:\\n            free = blocked.total - used.total\\n            self.available_token_capacity += free\\n\\n    def __del__(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Ensure live display is stopped on deletion.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if hasattr(self, \\"_live\\"):\\n            self._live.stop()\\n\\n    def estimate_request_cost(self, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> float:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate cost for a request based on token counts.\\"\\"\\"\\n        input_cost = (input_tokens * (self.input_cost_per_million or 0)) / 1_000_000\\n        output_cost = (output_tokens * (self.output_cost_per_million or 0)) / 1_000_000\\n        return input_cost + output_cost\\n\\n    def update_cost_projection(self, token_count: _TokenUsage | None, pre_request: bool = False):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Update cost projections based on token estimates or actual usage.\\"\\"\\"\\n        # Calculate estimated cost\\n        if token_count is None:\\n            estimated_cost = 0\\n        else:\\n            estimated_cost = self.estimate_request_cost(token_count.input, token_count.output)\\n\\n        if pre_request:\\n            # This is a new estimate before API call\\n            # Update moving average of estimates\\n            self.num_estimates += 1\\n            self.estimated_cost_average = (self.estimated_cost_average * (self.num_estimates - 1) + estimated_cost) / self.num_estimates\\n        else:\\n            # Decrement estimate count since we\'re getting actual results (success or failure)\\n            if self.num_estimates > 0:\\n                self.num_estimates -= 1\\n\\n        # Calculate remaining cost using current estimates and remaining requests\\n        remaining_requests = self.total_requests - (self.num_tasks_succeeded + self.num_tasks_failed + self.num_tasks_already_completed)\\n        if self.num_estimates > 0:\\n            in_flight_cost = self.estimated_cost_average * self.num_estimates\\n\\n            if self.num_tasks_succeeded > 0:\\n                # Calculate weighted average between actual and in-flight costs\\n                avg_actual_cost = self.total_cost / self.num_tasks_succeeded\\n\\n                total_weight = (self.num_tasks_succeeded * _SUCCESS_WEIGHT_FACTOR) + self.num_estimates\\n                weighted_avg_cost = ((avg_actual_cost * (self.num_tasks_succeeded * _SUCCESS_WEIGHT_FACTOR)) + in_flight_cost) / total_weight\\n\\n                # Calculate remaining cost using weighted average\\n                self.projected_remaining_cost = weighted_avg_cost * remaining_requests\\n            else:\\n                # If no successful requests, use average of in-flight estimates\\n                self.projected_remaining_cost = self.estimated_cost_average * remaining_requests\\n\\n        else:\\n            # No in-flight requests, use actual average if available\\n            if self.num_tasks_succeeded > 0:\\n                avg_actual_cost = self.total_cost / self.num_tasks_succeeded\\n                self.projected_remaining_cost = avg_actual_cost * remaining_requests\\n            else:\\n                # Fallback to the current estimate\\n                self.projected_remaining_cost = estimated_cost * remaining_requests\\n\\n        self.update_display()\\n\\n    def cost_info(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get cost information for the current request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\"total_requests\\": self.total_requests,\\n            \\"total_input_tokens\\": self.total_prompt_tokens,\\n            \\"total_output_tokens\\": self.total_completion_tokens,\\n            \\"total_cost_milli_dollars\\": self.total_cost * 1000,\\n        }",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None Can be:\\n        - None when there are errors\\n        - str for non-structured output\\n        - Dict[str, Any] for structured output\\n    response_errors: List of error messages. None when there are no errors.\\n    raw_response: The raw response data from the API.\\n    raw_request: The raw request data. inputs, outputs, and other metadata.\\n\\n    Attributes:\\n        task_id: Unique identifier for the request\\n        generic_request: The generic request object to be processed\\n        api_specific_request: The request formatted for the specific API\\n        attempts_left: Number of retry attempts remaining\\n        result: List to store results/errors from attempts\\n        prompt_formatter: Formatter for prompts and responses\\n        created_at: Timestamp when request was created\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    task_id: int\\n    generic_request: GenericRequest\\n    api_specific_request: dict\\n    attempts_left: int\\n    result: list = field(default_factory=list)\\n    prompt_formatter: PromptFormatter = field(default=None)\\n    created_at: datetime.datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.datetime.now)\\n\\n\\nclass BaseOnlineRequestProcessor(BaseRequestProcessor, ABC):\\n    \\"\\"\\"Abstract base class for online request processors that make real-time API calls.\\n\\n    This class handles rate limiting, retries, parallel processing and other common\\n    functionality needed for making real-time API requests.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: Configuration object containing settings for the request processor\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: OnlineRequestProcessorConfig):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize the BaseOnlineRequestProcessor.\\"\\"\\"\\n        super().__init__(config)\\n\\n        defaults = _DEFAULT_COST_MAP[\\"online\\"][\\"default\\"][\\"ratelimit\\"]\\n        self.token_limit_strategy = TokenLimitStrategy.default\\n        self.manual_max_requests_per_minute = config.max_requests_per_minute\\n        self.manual_max_tokens_per_minute = config.max_tokens_per_minute\\n        self.default_max_requests_per_minute = defaults[\\"max_requests_per_minute\\"]\\n        self.default_max_concurrent_requests = defaults[\\"max_concurrent_requests\\"]\\n        self.default_max_tokens_per_minute = defaults[\\"max_tokens_per_minute\\"][self.token_limit_strategy.value]\\n        self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute = None\\n        self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute = None\\n\\n        self.manual_max_concurrent_requests = config.max_concurrent_requests\\n        self.header_based_max_concurrent_requests = None\\n\\n        # The rich.Console used for the status tracker, only set for testing\\n        self._tracker_console = None\\n        self._output_tokens_window = deque(maxlen=_MAX_OUTPUT_MVA_WINDOW)\\n        self._semaphore = None\\n\\n    @property\\n    def backend(self) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Backend property.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return \\"base\\"\\n\\n    @property\\n    def compatible_provider(self) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Compatible provider property.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return \\"base\\"\\n\\n    def validate_config(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Validate online request processor configuration.\\n\\n        Ensures that configuration parameters are set correctly.\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            ValueError: If configuration parameters are invalid\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    def _unpack_multimodal(self, request: GenericRequest) -> GenericRequest:\\n        messages = request.messages\\n        if request.is_multimodal_prompt is True and self._multimodal_prompt_supported is False:\\n            raise TypeError(f\\"Multimodal prompts are not supported by this model {self.config.model}.\\")\\n\\n        if request.is_multimodal_prompt is False:\\n            return request\\n\\n        unpacked_messages = []\\n        for message in messages:\\n            # Load message content as multimodal prompt\\n            content = _MultiModalPrompt.model_validate(message[\\"content\\"])\\n            content = self._handle_multi_modal_prompt(content)\\n            message[\\"content\\"] = content\\n            unpacked_messages.append(message)\\n\\n        request.messages = unpacked_messages\\n        return request\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def file_upload_limit_check(self, base64_image: str) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if the image size is within the allowed limit.\\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    def _format_multimodal(self, data, mime_type=\\"image/png\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Format multimodal prompt data for API request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        mime_type = mime_type or \\"image/png\\"\\n        if data.url and not data.is_local:\\n            return {\\"type\\": \\"image_url\\", \\"image_url\\": {\\"url\\": data.url}}\\n        else:\\n            base64_content = data.serialize()\\n            self.file_upload_limit_check(base64_content)\\n\\n            content = {\\n                \\"type\\": \\"image_url\\",\\n                \\"image_url\\": {\\n                    \\"url\\": f\\"data:{mime_type};base64,{base64_content}\\",\\n                },\\n            }\\n            if \\"image\\" in mime_type:\\n                content[\\"image_url\\"].update({\\"detail\\": data.detail})\\n            return content\\n\\n    def _handle_multi_modal_prompt(self, message):\\n        content = []\\n        texts = message.texts\\n\\n        for text in texts:\\n            content.append({\\"type\\": \\"text\\", \\"text\\": text})\\n        for image in message.images:\\n            content.append(self._format_multimodal(image, mime_type=image.mime_type))\\n        for file in message.files:\\n            content.append(self._format_multimodal(file, mime_type=file.mime_type))\\n\\n        return content\\n\\n    @property\\n    def max_concurrent_requests(self) -> int | None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Gets the maximum concurrent requests rate limit.\\n\\n        Returns the manually set limit if available, falls back to header-based limit,\\n        or uses default value as last resort.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.manual_max_concurrent_requests:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Manually set max_concurrent_requests to {self.manual_max_concurrent_requests}\\")\\n            return self.manual_max_concurrent_requests\\n\\n        elif self.header_based_max_concurrent_requests:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Automatically set max_concurrent_requests to {self.header_based_max_concurrent_requests}\\")\\n            return self.header_based_max_concurrent_requests\\n        else:\\n            return None\\n\\n    @property\\n    def max_requests_per_minute(self) -> int:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Gets the maximum requests per minute rate limit.\\n\\n        Returns the manually set limit if available, falls back to header-based limit,\\n        or uses default value as last resort.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.manual_max_requests_per_minute:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Manually set max_requests_per_minute to {self.manual_max_requests_per_minute}\\")\\n            return self.manual_max_requests_per_minute\\n        elif self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Automatically set max_requests_per_minute to {self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute}\\")\\n            return self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute\\n        else:\\n            logger.warning(\\n self.default_max_requests_per_minute\\n\\n    @property\\n    def max_tokens_per_minute(self) -> int:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Gets the maximum tokens per minute rate limit.\\n\\n        Returns the manually set limit if available, falls back to header-based limit,\\n        or uses default value as last resort.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.manual_max_tokens_per_minute:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Manually set max_tokens_per_minute to {self.manual_max_tokens_per_minute}\\")\\n            return self.manual_max_tokens_per_minute\\n        elif self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute:\\n            logger.info(f\\"Automatically set max_tokens_per_minute to {self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute}\\")\\n            return self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute\\n        else:\\n            logger.warning(\\n                f\\"No manual max_tokens_per_minute set, and headers based detection failed, using default value of {self.default_max_tokens_per_minute}\\"\\n            )\\n            return self.default_max_tokens_per_minute\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def estimate_total_tokens(self, messages: list) -> _TokenUsage:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate total tokens for a request.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            messages: List of messages to estimate token count for\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Estimated total number of tokens\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def estimate_output_tokens(self) -> int:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate output tokens for a request.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Estimated number of output tokens\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def create_api_specific_request_online(self, generic_request: GenericRequest) -> dict:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create an API-specific request body from a generic request body.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            generic_request: The generic request to convert\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            API-specific request dictionary\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    def completion_cost(self, response):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Calculate the cost of a completion response using litellm.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            response: The completion response to calculate cost for\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Calculated cost of the completion\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Calculate cost using litellm\\n        cost = self._cost_processor.cost(completion_response=response)\\n        return cost\\n\\n    def requests_to_responses(\\n        self,\\n        generic_request_files: list[str],\\n    ) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Process multiple request files and generate corresponding response files.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            generic_request_files: List of request files to process\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        max_requests_per_minute = self.max_requests_per_minute\\n        max_tokens_per_minute = self.max_tokens_per_minute\\n\\n        self.tracker = OnlineStatusTracker(\\n            token_limit_strategy=self.token_limit_strategy,\\n            max_requests_per_minute=max_requests_per_minute,\\n            max_tokens_per_minute=max_tokens_per_minute,\\n            compatible_provider=self.compatible_provider,\\n            viewer_client=self._viewer_client,\\n            model=self.prompt_formatter.model_name,\\n            total_requests=self.total_requests,\\n        )\\n        for request_file in generic_request_files:\\n            response_file = request_file.replace(\\"requests_\\", \\"responses_\\")\\n            run_in_event_loop(\\n                self.process_requests_from_file(\\n                    generic_request_filepath=request_file,\\n                    response_file=response_file,\\n                    status_tracker=self.tracker,\\n                )\\n            )\\n\\n    async def cool_down_if_rate_limit_error(self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Pause processing if a rate limit error is detected.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            status_tracker: Tracker containing rate limit status\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        seconds_to_pause_on_rate_limit = self.config.seconds_to_pause_on_rate_limit\\n        seconds_since_rate_limit_error = time.time() - status_tracker.time_of_last_rate_limit_error\\n        remaining_seconds_to_pause = seconds_to_pause_on_rate_limit - seconds_since_rate_limit_error\\n        if remaining_seconds_to_pause > 0:\\n            logger.warning(f\\"Pausing for {int(remaining_seconds_to_pause)} seconds\\")\\n            await asyncio.sleep(remaining_seconds_to_pause)\\n            status_tracker.last_update_time = time.time()\\n\\n    def free_capacity(self, tracker, tokens):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Free blocked capacity.\\"\\"\\"\\n\\n    def aiohttp_connector(self, tcp_limit: int) -> aiohttp.ClientSession:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create an aiohttp connector with rate limiting.\\"\\"\\"\\n        connector = aiohttp.TCPConnector(limit=10 * tcp_limit)\\n        # trust_env=True makes aiohttp honor HTTP_PROXY/HTTPS_PROXY/ALL_PROXY/NO_PROXY,\\n        # which it otherwise ignores unlike requests/httpx (see issue #699).\\n        return aiohttp.ClientSession(connector=connector, trust_env=True)\\n\\n    async def process_requests_from_file(\\n        self,\\n        generic_request_filepath: str,\\n        response_file: str,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n    ) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Processes API requests in parallel, throttling to stay under rate limits.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            generic_request_filepath: Path to file containing requests\\n            response_file: Path where the response data will be saved\\n            status_tracker: Tracker containing rate limit status\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Initialize trackers\\n        queue_of_requests_to_retry: asyncio.Queue[APIRequest] = asyncio.Queue()\\n        if self.max_concurrent_requests is not None:\\n            self._semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(t.cast(int, self.max_concurrent_requests))\\n\\n        # Resume if a response file exists\\n        completed_request_ids, completed_parsed_responses = self.validate_existing_response_file(response_file)\\n\\n        # Count total requests\\n        status_tracker.num_tasks_already_completed = len(completed_request_ids)\\n        status_tracker.num_parsed_responses = completed_parsed_responses\\n        status_tracker.start_tracker(self._tracker_console)\\n\\n        # Use higher connector limit for better throughput\\n        tcp_limit = self.max_concurrent_requests if status_tracker.max_requests_per_minute is None else status_tracker.max_requests_per_minute\\n        # Update session status to inprogress\\n        await self._viewer_client.session_inprogress()\\n\\n        async with self.aiohttp_connector(tcp_limit) as session:\\n            async with aiofiles.open(generic_request_filepath) as file:\\n                pending_requests = []\\n                async for line in file:\\n                    if self._semaphore:\\n                        await self._semaphore.acquire()\\n\\n                    generic_request = GenericRequest.model_validate_json(line)\\n\\n                    if generic_request.original_row_idx in completed_request_ids:\\n                        if self._semaphore:\\n                            self._semaphore.release()\\n                        continue\\n\\n                    # Unpack multimodal prompts\\n                    generic_request = self._unpack_multimodal(generic_request)\\n\\n                    request = APIRequest(\\n                        task_id=status_tracker.num_tasks_started,\\n                        generic_request=generic_request,\\n                        api_specific_request=self.create_api_specific_request_online(generic_request),\\n                        attempts_left=self.config.max_retries,\\n                        prompt_formatter=self.prompt_formatter,\\n                    )\\n\\n                    if status_tracker.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n                        token_estimate = self.estimate_total_tokens(request.generic_request.messages)\\n                    else:\\n                        token_estimate = _TokenUsage()  # Empty\\n\\n                    # Wait for capacity if needed\\n                    while not status_tracker.has_capacity(token_estimate):\\n                        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\\n\\n                    # Wait for rate limits cool down if needed\\n                    await self.cool_down_if_rate_limit_error(status_tracker)\\n\\n                    # Consume capacity before making request\\n                    status_tracker.consume_capacity(token_estimate)\\n                    task = asyncio.create_task(\\n                        self.handle_single_request_with_retries(\\n                            request=request,\\n                            session=session,\\n                            retry_queue=queue_of_requests_to_retry,\\n                            response_file=response_file,\\n                            status_tracker=status_tracker,\\n                            blocked_capacity=token_estimate,\\n                        )\\n                    )\\n                    pending_requests.append(task)\\n\\n                    status_tracker.num_tasks_started += 1\\n                    status_tracker.num_tasks_in_progress += 1\\n\\n            if pending_requests:\\n                await asyncio.gather(*pending_requests)\\n\\n            # Process any remaining retries in the queue\\n            pending_retries = set()\\n            while not queue_of_requests_to_retry.empty() or pending_retries:\\n                # Process new items from the queue if we have capacity\\n                if self._semaphore:\\n                    await self._semaphore.acquire()\\n\\n                if not queue_of_requests_to_retry.empty():\\n                    retry_request = await queue_of_requests_to_retry.get()\\n                    if status_tracker.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n                        token_estimate = self.estimate_total_tokens(retry_request.generic_request.messages)\\n                    else:\\n                        token_estimate = _TokenUsage()  # Empty\\n\\n                    attempt_number = self.config.max_retries - retry_request.attempts_left\\n                    logger.debug(\\n {retry_request.result}\\"\\n                    )\\n\\n                    # Wait for capacity if needed\\n                    while not status_tracker.has_capacity(token_estimate):\\n                        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\\n\\n                    # Wait for rate limits cool down if needed\\n                    await self.cool_down_if_rate_limit_error(status_tracker)\\n\\n                    # Consume capacity before making request\\n                    status_tracker.consume_capacity(token_estimate)\\n\\n                    task = asyncio.create_task(\\n                        self.handle_single_request_with_retries(\\n                            request=retry_request,\\n                            session=session,\\n                            retry_queue=queue_of_requests_to_retry,\\n                            response_file=response_file,\\n                            status_tracker=status_tracker,\\n                            blocked_capacity=token_estimate,\\n                        )\\n                    )\\n                    pending_retries.add(task)\\n\\n                # Wait for some tasks to complete\\n                if pending_retries:\\n                    done, pending_retries = await asyncio.wait(pending_retries, timeout=0.1)\\n\\n        status_tracker.stop_tracker()\\n        await self.viewer_client.session_completed()\\n\\n        # Log cost projection to viewer\\n        await self.viewer_client.log_cost_projection(status_tracker, force_log=True)\\n\\n        # Log final status\\n        logger.info(f\\"Processing complete. Results saved to {response_file}\\")\\n        logger.info(f\\"Status tracker: {status_tracker}\\")\\n\\n        if status_tracker.num_tasks_failed > 0:\\n            logger.warning(f\\"{status_tracker.num_tasks_failed} / {status_tracker.num_tasks_started} requests failed. Errors logged to {response_file}.\\")\\n\\n    def _free_capacity(self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, used_capacity: \\"_TokenUsage\\", blocked_capacity: \\"_TokenUsage\\"):\\n        if status_tracker.max_tokens_per_minute is not None:\\n            status_tracker.free_capacity(used_capacity, blocked_capacity)\\n\\n    async def handle_single_request_with_retries(\\n        self,\\n        request: APIRequest,\\n        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,\\n        retry_queue: asyncio.Queue,\\n        response_file: str,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n        blocked_capacity: \\"_TokenUsage\\",\\n    ) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Common wrapper for handling a single request with error handling and retries.\\n\\n        This method implements the common try/except logic and retry mechanism,\\n        while delegating the actual API call to call_single_request.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request: The request to process\\n            session: Async HTTP session\\n            retry_queue: Queue for failed requests\\n            response_file: Path where the response data will be saved\\n            status_tracker: Tracks request status\\n            blocked_capacity: Blocked token capacity\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        try:\\n            # Estimate tokens before making request\\n            generic_response = None\\n            token_estimate: _TokenUsage = blocked_capacity or self.estimate_total_tokens(request.generic_request.messages)\\n\\n            # Add new estimate to projection (pre_request=True indicates new estimate)\\n            status_tracker.update_cost_projection(token_estimate, pre_request=True)\\n\\n            generic_response = await self.call_single_request(\\n                request=request,\\n                session=session,\\n                status_tracker=status_tracker,\\n            )\\n\\n            if generic_response.finish_reason in self.config.invalid_finish_reasons:\\n                logger.debug(\\n                    f\\"Invalid finish_reason {generic_response.finish_reason}.\\"\\n                    \\" Raw response {generic_response.raw_response} \\"\\n                    \\"for request {generic_response.raw_request}\\"\\n                )\\n                # Update cost projection with actual usage but mark as failed\\n                status_tracker.update_stats(generic_response.token_usage, generic_response.response_cost)\\n                raise ValueError(f\\"finish_reason was {generic_response.finish_reason}\\")\\n\\n            # Allows us to retry on responses that don\'t match the response format\\n            self.prompt_formatter.response_to_response_format(generic_response.response_message)\\n\\n            # Log cost projection to viewer\\n            await self._viewer_client.log_cost_projection(status_tracker)\\n\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            status_tracker.num_other_errors += 1\\n            request.result.append(e)\\n\\n            if generic_response and generic_response.token_usage is not None:\\n                used_tokens: _TokenUsage = _TokenUsage(input=generic_response.token_usage.input, output=generic_response.token_usage.output)\\n                status_tracker.update_cost_projection(used_tokens)\\n            else:\\n                status_tracker.update_cost_projection(None)\\n\\n            if request.attempts_left > 0:\\n                request.attempts_left -= 1\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    f\\"Encountered \'{e.__class__.__name__}: {e}\' during attempt \\"\\n                    f\\"{self.config.max_retries - request.attempts_left} of {self.config.max_retries} \\"\\n                    f\\"while processing request {request.task_id}\\"\\n                )\\n                retry_queue.put_nowait(request)\\n            else:\\n                error_counts = Counter(str(err) for err in request.result)\\n                formatted_errors = [f\\"{error}(x{count})\\" for error, count in error_counts.items()]\\n                logger.error(\\n                    f\\"Request {request.task_id} failed permanently after exhausting all {self.config.max_retries} retry attempts. \\"\\n                    f\\"Errors: {formatted_errors}\\"\\n                )\\n\\n                generic_response = GenericResponse(\\n                    response_message=None,\\n                    response_errors=formatted_errors,\\n                    raw_request=request.api_specific_request,\\n                    raw_response=None,\\n                    generic_request=request.generic_request,\\n                    created_at=request.created_at,\\n                    finished_at=datetime.datetime.now(),\\n                )\\n                await self.append_generic_response(status_tracker, generic_response, response_file)\\n                status_tracker.num_tasks_in_progress -= 1\\n                status_tracker.num_tasks_failed += 1\\n            return\\n        else:\\n            self._add_output_token_moving_window(generic_response.token_usage.output)\\n        finally:\\n            if self._semaphore:\\n                self._semaphore.release()\\n\\n        # Save response in the base class\\n        await self.append_generic_response(status_tracker, generic_response, response_file)\\n\\n        status_tracker.num_tasks_in_progress -= 1\\n        status_tracker.num_tasks_succeeded += 1\\n\\n        # Update cost projection with actual usage\\n        used_tokens: _TokenUsage = _TokenUsage(input=generic_response.token_usage.input, output=generic_response.token_usage.output)\\n        # Update stats with actual usage\\n        status_tracker.update_stats(generic_response.token_usage, generic_response.response_cost)\\n        status_tracker.update_cost_projection(used_tokens)\\n\\n        # Free the extra capacity blocked before request with actual consumed capacity.\\n        self._free_capacity(status_tracker, used_tokens, blocked_capacity)\\n\\n    def _add_output_token_moving_window(self, tokens):\\n        self._output_tokens_window.append(tokens)\\n\\n    def _output_tokens_moving_average(self):\\n        return sum(self._output_tokens_window) / (len(self._output_tokens_window) or _MAX_OUTPUT_MVA_WINDOW)\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    async def call_single_request(\\n        self,\\n        request: APIRequest,\\n        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n    ) -> GenericResponse:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Make a single API request without error handling.\\n\\n        This method should implement the actual API call logic\\n        without handling retries or errors.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request: Request to process\\n            session: Async HTTP session\\n            status_tracker: Tracks request status\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            The response from the API call\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n    async def append_generic_response(self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, data: GenericResponse, filename: str) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Append a response to a jsonl file with async file operations.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            status_tracker: Tracker containing request status\\n            data: Response data to append\\n            filename: File to append to\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        raw_response = data.response_message\\n        responses = self._process_response(data)\\n        if not responses:\\n            return\\n        data.parsed_response_message = responses\\n        data.response_message = raw_response\\n        data_dump = data.model_dump()\\n        json_string = json.dumps(data_dump, default=str)\\n        async with aiofiles.open(filename, \\"a\\") as f:\\n            await f.write(json_string + \\"\\\\n\\")\\n        logger.debug(f\\"Successfully appended response to {filename}\\")\\n        filename = os.path.basename(filename).split(\\".\\")[0]\\n        idx = status_tracker.num_parsed_responses\\n        status_tracker.num_parsed_responses = idx + len(responses)\\n        await self.viewer_client.stream_response(json_string, idx)",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] _DEFAULT_COMPLETION_SUFFIX = \\"/chat/completions\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: OnlineRequestProcessorConfig, compatible_provider: str = None):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Initialize the limits results in a 400 error like this:\\n            # {\'error\': {\'message\': \'Empty input messages\', \'type\': \'invalid_request_error\', \'param\': None, \'code\': \'invalid_request_error\'}}\\n            self.api_key = self.config.api_key or os.getenv(\\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\\")\\n            self._longlived_response = True\\n            self.config.request_timeout = 60 * 30  # 30 minutes\\n            self.manual_max_concurrent_requests = config.max_concurrent_requests or 10_000\\n            self.default_max_tokens_per_minute = None\\n        else:\\n            self.api_key = self.config.api_key or os.getenv(\\"OPENAI_API_KEY\\")\\n            self.header_based_max_requests_per_minute, self.header_based_max_tokens_per_minute = self.get_header_based_rate_limits()\\n        self.token_encoding = self.get_token_encoding()\\n\\n    @property\\n    def backend(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Backend property.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return \\"openai\\"\\n\\n    @property\\n    def compatible_provider(self) -> str:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Compatible provider property.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return self._compatible_provider\\n\\n    def aiohttp_connector(self, tcp_limit: int) -> aiohttp.ClientSession:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create an aiohttp connector with rate limiting.\\"\\"\\"\\n        if self._longlived_response:\\n            connector = aiohttp.TCPConnector(limit=10 * tcp_limit, keepalive_timeout=self.config.request_timeout)\\n            timeout = aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=self.config.request_timeout, connect=10, sock_connect=10, sock_read=self.config.request_timeout)\\n            return aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=timeout, connector=connector)\\n        else:\\n            return super().aiohttp_connector(tcp_limit)\\n\\n    def get_header_based_rate_limits(self) -> tuple[int, int]:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get rate limits from OpenAI API headers.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            tuple[int, int]: Contains \'max_requests_per_minute\' and \'max_tokens_per_minute\'\\n\\n        Note:\\n            - Makes a dummy request to get actual rate limits\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if not self.api_key:\\n            raise ValueError(\\"Missing OpenAI API Key - Please set OPENAI_API_KEY in your environment vars\\")\\n\\n        response = requests.post(\\n            self.url,\\n            headers={\\"Authorization\\": f\\"Bearer {self.api_key}\\"},\\n            json={\\"model\\": self.config.model, \\"messages\\": []},\\n        )\\n        from bespokelabs.curator.cost import RATE_LIMIT_HEADER\\n\\n        for provider in RATE_LIMIT_HEADER:\\n            if provider in self.url:\\n                rate_limit_header = RATE_LIMIT_HEADER[provider]\\n                rpm_key = rate_limit_header[\\"request-key\\"]\\n                tpm_key = rate_limit_header[\\"token-key\\"]\\n                rpm = float(response.headers.get(rpm_key[\\"key\\"], 0))\\n                tpm = float(response.headers.get(tpm_key[\\"key\\"], 0))\\n                if rpm_key.get(\\"type\\") == \\"rps\\":\\n                    rpm = rpm * 60\\n                if tpm_key.get(\\"type\\") == \\"tps\\":\\n                    tpm = tpm * 60\\n                return int(rpm), int(tpm)\\n\\n        rpm = int(response.headers.get(\\"x-ratelimit-limit-requests\\", 0))\\n        tpm = int(response.headers.get(\\"x-ratelimit-limit-tokens\\", 0))\\n\\n        return rpm, tpm\\n\\n    def estimate_output_tokens(self) -> int:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate number of tokens in the response.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            int: Estimated number of output tokens\\n\\n        Note:\\n            Default implementation returns a conservative estimate.\\n            Override this method for more accurate model-specific estimates.\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        if self.config.model in litellm.model_cost:\\n            return litellm.get_max_tokens(model=self.config.model) // 4\\n        else:\\n            return 4096\\n\\n    def estimate_total_tokens(self, messages: list) -> _TokenUsage:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Estimate total tokens for a request using OpenAI\'s token counting rules.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            messages (list): List of message dictionaries with role and content\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            _TokenUsage: Estimated input and output tokens including message formatting tokens\\n\\n        Note:\\n            Includes:\\n            - 4 tokens per message for formatting\\n            - Role/name tokens\\n            - Content tokens\\n            - 2 tokens for assistant reply priming\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        num_tokens = 0\\n        for message in messages:\\n            num_tokens += 4  # every message follows <im_start>{role/name}\\\\n{content}<im_end>\\\\n\\n            for key, value in message.items():\\n                try:\\n                    num_tokens += openai_request_mixin.calculate_input_tokens(value, self.token_encoding)\\n                except TypeError:\\n                    logger.warning(f\\"Failed to encode value {value} with tiktoken.
