num_tokens -= 1  # role is always required and always 1 token\\n\\n        num_tokens += 2  # every reply is primed with <im_start>assistant\\n        output_tokens = self.estimate_output_tokens()\\n        return _TokenUsage(input=num_tokens, output=output_tokens)\\n\\n    def check_structured_output_support(self) -> bool:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if the model supports structured output based on model name via litellm.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            bool: True if model supports structured output, False otherwise\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        model_name = self.config.model.lower()\\n\\n        if model_name in [\\"deepseek-reasoner\\", \\"deepseek-chat\\"] and \\"api.deepseek.com\\" in self.url:\\n            return True\\n        if self.config.base_url is not None:\\n            return self._check_structured_output_support_via_api()\\n        from litellm import supports_response_schema\\n\\n        # Check if model supports Pydantic models / json_schema\\n        return supports_response_schema(model=model_name)\\n\\n    def _check_structured_output_support_via_api(self) -> bool:\\n        class User(BaseModel):\\n            name: str\\n            age: int\\n\\n        try:\\n            client = openai.OpenAI(api_key=self.api_key, base_url=self.config.base_url)\\n            client.beta.chat.completions.parse(\\n                model=self.config.model,\\n                messages=[\\n                    {\\n                        \\"role\\": \\"user\\",\\n                        \\"content\\": \\"Jason is 25 years old.\\",\\n                    },\\n                ],\\n                response_format=User,\\n            )\\n            logger.info(f\\"Model {self.config.model} supports structured output via OpenAI functions\\")\\n            return True\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            logger.warning(f\\"Model {self.config.model} does not support structured output via OpenAI functions: {e}\\")\\n            return False\\n\\n    def file_upload_limit_check(self, base64_image: str) -> None:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Check if the image size is within the allowed limit.\\"\\"\\"\\n        mb = get_base64_size(base64_image)\\n        if mb > _OPENAI_ALLOWED_IMAGE_SIZE_MB:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"Image size is {mb} MB, which is greater than the allowed size of {_OPENAI_ALLOWED_IMAGE_SIZE_MB} MB.\\")\\n\\n    @property\\n    def _multimodal_prompt_supported(self) -> bool:\\n        return True\\n\\n    def create_api_specific_request_online(self, generic_request: GenericRequest) -> dict:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Create an OpenAI-specific request from a generic request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return OpenAIRequestMixin.create_api_specific_request_online(self, generic_request)\\n\\n    async def call_single_request(\\n        self,\\n        request: APIRequest,\\n        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n    ) -> GenericResponse:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Make a single OpenAI API request.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request (APIRequest): The request to process\\n            session (aiohttp.ClientSession): Async HTTP session\\n            status_tracker (OnlineStatusTracker): Tracks request status\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            GenericResponse: The response from OpenAI\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        request_header = {\\"Authorization\\": f\\"Bearer {self.api_key}\\"}\\n        if \\"/deployments\\" in self.url:  # Azure deployment\\n            request_header = {\\"api-key\\": f\\"{self.api_key}\\"}\\n        response = await fetch_response(\\n            session,\\n            url=self.url,\\n            headers=request_header,\\n            payload=request.api_specific_request,\\n            timeout=self.config.request_timeout,\\n            longlived_response=self._longlived_response,\\n        )\\n\\n        if response is None:\\n            raise Exception(\\"Response is empty\\")\\n        elif \\"error\\" in response:\\n            status_tracker.num_api_errors += 1\\n            error = response[\\"error\\"]\\n            error_message = error if isinstance(error, str) else error.get(\\"message\\", \\"\\")\\n            if \\"rate limit\\" in error_message.lower():\\n                status_tracker.time_of_last_rate_limit_error = time.time()\\n                status_tracker.num_rate_limit_errors += 1\\n                status_tracker.num_api_errors -= 1\\n                # because handle_single_request_with_retries will double count otherwise\\n                status_tracker.num_other_errors -= 1\\n            raise Exception(f\\"API error: {error}\\")\\n\\n        if self.config.return_completions_object:\\n            response_message = dict(response)\\n        else:\\n            response_message = response[\\"choices\\"][0][\\"message\\"][\\"content\\"]\\n        finish_reason = response[\\"choices\\"][0].get(\\"finish_reason\\", \\"unknown\\")\\n        usage = response[\\"usage\\"]\\n        token_usage = _TokenUsage(\\n            input=usage[\\"prompt_tokens\\"],\\n            output=usage[\\"completion_tokens\\"],\\n            total=usage[\\"total_tokens\\"],\\n        )\\n\\n        cost = self.completion_cost(response)\\n\\n        # Create and return response\\n        return GenericResponse(\\n            response_message=response_message,\\n            response_errors=None,\\n            raw_request=request.api_specific_request,\\n            raw_response=response,\\n            generic_request=request.generic_request,\\n            created_at=request.created_at,\\n            finished_at=datetime.datetime.now(),\\n            token_usage=token_usage,\\n            response_cost=cost,\\n            finish_reason=finish_reason,\\n        )\\n\\n    def get_token_encoding(self):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Get the token encoding for the model.\\"\\"\\"\\n        return tiktoken.get_encoding(\\"cl100k_base\\")",\n  "error": "",\n  "base64_image": null,\n  "system": null\n}', annotations=None, meta=None)] structuredContent=None isError=False

response.headers\\n\\n    def _format_multimodal(self, data, mime_type=\\"image/png\\"):\\n        \\"\\"\\"Format multimodal prompt data for API request.\\"\\"\\"\\n        mime_type = mime_type or \\"image/png\\"\\n        if data.url and not data.is_local:\\n            return {\\"type\\": \\"image\\", \\"source\\": {\\"type\\": \\"url\\", \\"url\\": data.url}}\\n        else:\\n            base64_content = data.serialize()\\n            self.file_upload_limit_check(base64_content)\\n\\n            content = {\\n                \\"type\\": \\"image\\",\\n                \\"source\\": {\\n                    \\"type\\": \\"base64\\",\\n                    \\"media_type\\": mime_type,\\n                    \\"data\\": base64_content,\\n                },\\n            }\\n           if any(pattern in model for pattern in json_supported_patterns):\\n                request[\\"response_format\\"] = {\\"type\\": \\"json_schema\\", \\"schema\\": generic_request.response_format}\\n\\n        # Handle thinking parameter if provided\\n        if \\"thinking\\" in generic_request.generation_params:\\n            request[\\"thinking\\"] = generic_request.generation_params.pop(\\"thinking\\")\\n\\n        # Copy over remaining generation parameters\\n        for key, value in generic_request.generation_params.items():\\n            if key != \\"max_tokens\\":  # Already handled above\\n                request[key] = value\\n\\n        return request\\n\\n    async def call_single_request(\\n        self,\\n        request: APIRequest,\\n        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n    ) -> GenericResponse:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Make a single Anthropic API request.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request (APIRequest): The request to process\\n            session (aiohttp.ClientSession): Async HTTP session\\n            status_tracker (OnlineStatusTracker): Tracks request status\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            GenericResponse: The response from Anthropic\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        headers = {\\n            \\"x-api-key\\": self.api_key,\\n            \\"anthropic-version\\": \\"2023-06-01\\",\\n            \\"content-type\\": \\"application/json\\",\\n        }\\n\\n        async with session.post(\\n            self.url,\\n            headers=headers,\\n            json=request.api_specific_request,\\n            timeout=self.config.request_timeout,\\n        ) as response_obj:\\n            response = await response_obj.json()\\n\\n            if response is None:\\n                raise Exception(\\"Response is empty\\")\\n            elif \\"error\\" in response:\\n                status_tracker.num_api_errors += 1\\n                error = response[\\"error\\"]\\n                error_message = error.get(\\"message\\", str(error))\\n                if \\"rate limit\\" in error_message.lower():\\n                    status_tracker.time_of_last_rate_limit_error = time.time()\\n                    status_tracker.num_rate_limit_errors += 1\\n                    status_tracker.num_api_errors -= 1\\n                    # because handle_single_request_with_retries will double count otherwise\\n                    status_tracker.num_other_errors -= 1\\n                raise Exception(f\\"API error: {error}\\")\\n\\n            if response_obj.status != 200:\\n                raise Exception(f\\"API request failed with status {response_obj.status}: {response}\\")\\n\\n            # Handle response based on return_completions_object flag\\n            if self.config.return_completions_object:\\n                response_message = dict(response)\\n            else:\\n                # Extract content from the first text content block\\n                content_blocks = response.get(\\"content\\", [])\\n                if content_blocks:\\n                    # Find the first text content block\\n                    for block in content_blocks:\\n                        if block.get(\\"type\\") == \\"text\\":\\n                            response_message = block.get(\\"text\\", \\"\\")\\n                            break\\n                    else:\\n                        # If no text content block found, return the whole message\\n                        response_message = str(content_blocks)\\n                else:\\n                    response_message = \\"\\"\\n\\n            # Get usage information\\n            usage = response.get(\\"usage\\", {})\\n            token_usage = _TokenUsage(\\n                input=usage.get(\\"input_tokens\\", 0),\\n                output=usage.get(\\"output_tokens\\", 0),\\n Calculate cost\\n            cost = self.completion_cost(response)\\n\\n            # Create and return response\\n            return GenericResponse(\\n generic_request=request.generic_request,\\n                created_at=request.created_at,\\n                finished_at=datetime.datetime.now(),\\n                token_usage=token_usage,\\n response headers.\\n            - Some providers (e.g., Claude) require non-empty messages\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        logger.info(f\\"Getting rate limits for model: {self.config.model}\\")\\n\\n        headers = self.test_call()\\n        provider = self.config.model.split(\\"/\\")[0]\\n        output_token_key = f\\"llm_provider-{provider}-ratelimit-output-tokens-remaining\\"\\n        rpm = int(headers.get(\\"x-ratelimit-limit-requests\\", 0))\\n        if output_token_key in headers:\\n            self.token_limit_strategy = TokenLimitStrategy.seperate\\n            output_tpm = int(headers.get(output_token_key, 0))\\n\\n            input_token_key = f\\"llm_provider-{provider}-ratelimit-input-tokens-remaining\\"\\n            input_tpm = int(headers.get(input_token_key, 0))\\n            tpm = _TokenUsage(input=input_tpm, output=output_tpm)\\n\\n        else:\\n            tpm = int(headers.get(\\"x-ratelimit-limit-tokens\\", 0))\\n\\n        return rpm, tpm\\n\\n    def create_api_specific_request_online(self, generic_request: GenericRequest) -> dict:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Convert a generic request into a LiteLLM-compatible format.\\n\\n        Checks supported parameters for the specific model and only includes\\n        applicable parameters.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            generic_request (GenericRequest): The generic request to convert\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            dict: LiteLLM-compatible request parameters\\n\\n        Note:\\n            Uses LiteLLM\'s get_supported_openai_params to check parameter support\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        request = {\\n            \\"model\\": generic_request.model,\\n            \\"messages\\": generic_request.messages,\\n        }\\n\\n        for key, value in generic_request.generation_params.items():\\n            request[key] = value\\n\\n        # Pass api_base per-request instead of globally\\n        if self.config.base_url is not None:\\n            request[\\"api_base\\"] = self.config.base_url\\n\\n        # Add safety settings for Gemini models\\n        if \\"gemini\\" in generic_request.model.lower():\\n            request[\\"safety_settings\\"] = [\\n                {\\n                    \\"category\\": \\"HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT\\",\\n                    \\"threshold\\": \\"BLOCK_NONE\\",\\n                },\\n                {\\n                    \\"category\\": \\"HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH\\",\\n                    \\"threshold\\": \\"BLOCK_NONE\\",\\n                },\\n                {\\n                    \\"category\\": \\"HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT\\",\\n                    \\"threshold\\": \\"BLOCK_NONE\\",\\n                },\\n                {\\n                    \\"category\\": \\"HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT\\",\\n                    \\"threshold\\": \\"BLOCK_NONE\\",\\n                },\\n                {\\n                    \\"category\\": \\"HARM_CATEGORY_CIVIC_INTEGRITY\\",\\n                    \\"threshold\\": \\"BLOCK_NONE\\",\\n                },\\n            ]\\n\\n        if self._uses_anthropic_multimodal_format(generic_request.model) and self._has_anthropic_pdf_document(generic_request.messages):\\n            request[\\"extra_headers\\"] = {\\n                **request.get(\\"extra_headers\\", {}),\\n                **_ANTHROPIC_PDF_BETA_HEADER,\\n            }\\n\\n        return request\\n\\n    async def call_single_request(\\n        self,\\n        request: APIRequest,\\n        session: aiohttp.ClientSession,\\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\\n    ) -> GenericResponse:\\n        \\"\\"\\"Make a single request through LiteLLM.\\n\\n        Handles both structured and unstructured outputs, tracks token usage\\n        and costs.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            request (APIRequest): Request to process\\n            session (aiohttp.ClientSession): Async HTTP session\\n            status_tracker (OnlineStatusTracker): Tracks request status\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            GenericResponse: The response from LiteLLM\\n        \\"\\"\\"\\n        # Get response directly without extra logging\\n        try:\\n            if request.generic_request.response_format:\\n                (\\n                    response,\\n                    completion_obj,\\n                ) = await self.client.chat.completions.create_with_completion(\\n                    **request.api_specific_request,\\n                    response_model=request.prompt_formatter.response_format,\\n                    timeout=self.config.request_timeout,\\n                )\\n                response_message = response.model_dump() if hasattr(response, \\"model_dump\\") else response\\n            else:\\n                completion_obj = await litellm.acompletion(**request.api_specific_request, timeout=self.config.request_timeout)\\n                if completion_obj is None:\\n                    raise Exception(\\"Response is empty\\")\\n                if self.config.return_completions_object:\\n                    response_message = dict(completion_obj)\\n                else:\\n                    response_message = completion_obj[\\"choices\\"][0][\\"message\\"][\\"content\\"]\\n        except litellm.RateLimitError as e:\\n            status_tracker.time_of_last_rate_limit_error = time.time()\\n            status_tracker.num_rate_limit_errors += 1\\n            # because handle_single_request_with_retries will double count otherwise\\n            status_tracker.num_api_errors -= 1\\n            raise e\\n\\n        # Extract token usage\\n        usage = completion_obj.usage if hasattr(completion_obj, \\"usage\\") else {}\\n        token_usage = _TokenUsage(\\n            input=usage.prompt_tokens,\\n            output=usage.completion_tokens,\\n            total=usage.total_tokens,\\n        )\\n\\n        cost = self.completion_cost(completion_obj.model_dump())\\n\\n        finish_reason = completion_obj.choices[0].finish_reason\\n\\n        if response_message is None:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\"response_message was None with raw response {completion_obj.model_dump()}\\")\\n\\n        # Create and return response\\n        return GenericResponse(\\n            response_message=response_message,\\n            response_errors=None,\\n            raw_request=request.api_specific_request,\\n            raw_response=completion_obj.model_dump(),\\n            generic_request=request.generic_request,\\n rate-limit headers and provides the constants / types the\nonline status tracker needs to seed, refill and settle per-minute buckets.\n"""\n\nimport math\nimport re\nimport typing as t\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom enum import Enum\n\nfrom bespokelabs.curator.types.token_usage import _TokenUsage\n\n# Default per-minute limits used when a configured limit is the sentinel 0.\nDEFAULT_MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE = 200\nDEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE_COMBINED = 100_000\nDEFAULT_MAX_INPUT_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 100_000\nDEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 40_000\n\n# Origin labels for the limit axes.\nLIMIT_ORIGIN_CONFIGURED = "configured"\nLIMIT_ORIGIN_DEFAULTED = "defaulted"\nLIMIT_ORIGIN_UNLIMITED = "unlimited"\n\n# Debt floor: a bucket may go as low as -limit * CAPACITY_DEBT_FLOOR_FRACTION.\nCAPACITY_DEBT_FLOOR_FRACTION: float = 0.25\n\n# Header-name tuples consulted, in order, first parseable wins.\nREQUEST_LIMIT_HEADERS = (\n    "x-ratelimit-limit-requests",\n    "anthropic-ratelimit-requests-limit",\n)\nINPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = (\n    "x-ratelimit-limit-input-tokens",\n    "anthropic-ratelimit-input-tokens-limit",\n)\nOUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = (\n    "x-ratelimit-limit-output-tokens",\n    "anthropic-ratelimit-output-tokens-limit",\n)\nTOTAL_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS = (\n    "x-ratelimit-limit-tokens",\n    "anthropic-ratelimit-tokens-limit",\n)\n\n\nclass TokenLimitStrategy(str, Enum):\n    """Token limit Strategy enum."""\n\n    combined = "combined"\n    seperate = "seperate"\n    default = "combined"\n\n\nclass CapacityExceedsLimitError(ValueError):\n    """Raised when a single request could never fit within the per-minute limit."""\n\n    def __init__(self, axis: str, requested: int, limit: float):\n        self.axis = axis\n        self.requested = requested\n        self.limit = limit\n        super().__init__(\n            f"request needs {requested} {axis} capacity but the per-minute limit is {int(limit)}"\n        )\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:\n        return f"request needs {self.requested} {self.axis} capacity but the per-minute limit is {int(self.limit)}"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass RateLimitReading:\n    """Decoded view of a provider\'s rate-limit response headers."""\n\n    max_requests_per_minute: int | None\n    max_tokens_per_minute: int | _TokenUsage | None\n    token_limit_strategy: TokenLimitStrategy\n    source_headers: tuple[str, ...]\n\n\n_NUMERIC_RE = re.compile(r"^(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)([kKmM]?)$")\n\n\ndef parse_limit_value(raw: object) -> int | None:\n    """Decode a single rate-limit header value.\n\n    ``bool`` is rejected even though it is an ``int`` subclass.  A positive\n    ``int`` returns itself.  A ``str`` is stripped and matched against a\n    ``<digits>[.<digits>][kKmM]`` pattern, floored to an int.  Everything else\n    yields ``None`` -- nothing raises.\n    """\n    if isinstance(raw, bool):\n        return None\n    if isinstance(raw, int):\n        return raw if raw > 0 else None\n    if isinstance(raw, str):\n        m = _NUMERIC_RE.match(raw.strip())\n        if m is None:\n            return None\n        number = float(m.group(1))\n        suffix = m.group(2)\n        if suffix in ("k", "K"):\n            number *= 1_000\n        elif suffix in ("m", "M"):\n            number *= 1_000_000\n        value = math.floor(number)\n        return value if value > 0 else None\n    return None\n\n\ndef _first_parseable(\n    lowered: t.Mapping[str, object], names: tuple[str, ...]\n) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    """Return ``(header_name, parsed_value)`` for the first parseable name."""\n    for name in names:\n        if name in lowered:\n            parsed = parse_limit_value(lowered[name])\n            if parsed is not None:\n                return name, parsed\n    return None, None\n\n\ndef read_rate_limit_headers(headers: t.Mapping[str, object]) -> RateLimitReading:\n    """Decode provider rate-limit headers into a :class:`RateLimitReading`.\n\n    Lower-cases the incoming keys once, then consults only the four name tuples\n    above in order.  No ``*-remaining`` / ``*-reset`` / ``llm_provider-*`` key\n    is ever read, and there are no hardcoded numeric fallbacks.\n    """\n    lowered: dict[str, object] = {k.lower(): v for k, v in headers.items()}\n\n    req_name, rpm = _first_parseable(lowered, REQUEST_LIMIT_HEADERS)\n    in_name, input_tpm = _first_parseable(lowered, INPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS)\n    out_name, output_tpm = _first_parseable(lowered, OUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS)\n    total_name, total_tpm = _first_parseable(lowered, TOTAL_TOKEN_LIMIT_HEADERS)\n\n    source: list[str] = []\n    if req_name is not None:\n        source.append(req_name)\n\n    if input_tpm is not None and output_tpm is not None:\n        # separate input/output accounting; a total header, if any, is ignored.\n        source.append(in_name)\n        source.append(out_name)\n        return RateLimitReading(\n            max_requests_per_minute=rpm,\n            max_tokens_per_minute=_TokenUsage(input=input_tpm, output=output_tpm),\n            token_limit_strategy=TokenLimitStrategy.seperate,\n            source_headers=tuple(source),\n        )\n\n    # combined accounting using the total-token header (if usable); a lone half\n    # of the input/output pair is discarded.\n    if total_tpm is not None:\n        source.append(total_name)\n        return RateLimitReading(\n            max_requests_per_minute=rpm,\n            max_tokens_per_minute=total_tpm,\n            token_limit_strategy=TokenLimitStrategy.combined,\n            source_headers=tuple(source),\n        )\n\n    # Nothing usable for tokens -> combined with None per the spec.\n    return RateLimitReading(\n        max_requests_per_minute=rpm,\n        max_tokens_per_minute=None,\n        token_limit_strategy=TokenLimitStrategy.combined,\n        source_headers=tuple(source),\n    )\n'}

str_replace_editor: {'command': 'str_replace', 'new_str': '        # Calculate cost using litellm\n        cost = self._cost_processor.cost(completion_response=response)\n        return cost\n\n    def _reserve_capacity(\n        self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, messages: list\n    ) -> _TokenUsage | None:\n        """Estimate tokens, check capacity and consume it on success.\n\n        Calls ``estimate_total_tokens`` exactly once and unconditionally --\n        including when ``status_tracker.max_tokens_per_minute is None`` -- then\n        ``status_tracker.has_capacity(estimate)`` (letting\n        ``CapacityExceedsLimitError`` propagate). On ``True`` it consumes and\n        returns the very ``_TokenUsage`` object it estimated, on ``False`` it\n        returns ``None`` having consumed nothing, neither tokens nor the request\n        slot.\n        """\n        estimate = self.estimate_total_tokens(messages)\n        if status_tracker.has_capacity(estimate):\n            status_tracker.consume_capacity(estimate)\n            return estimate\n        return None\n\n    def _free_capacity(\n        self,\n        status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker,\n        used_capacity: _TokenUsage,\n        blocked_capacity: _TokenUsage,\n    ) -> None:\n        """Delegate to the tracker\'s settlement."""\n        status_tracker.free_capacity(used_capacity, blocked_capacity)\n\n    def _refund_capacity(\n        self, status_tracker: OnlineStatusTracker, blocked_capacity: _TokenUsage\n    ) -> None:\n        """Delegate to the tracker\'s refund."""\n        status_tracker.refund_capacity(blocked_capacity)\n\n', 'old_str': '        # Calculate cost using litellm\n        cost = self._cost_processor.cost(completion_response=response)\n        return cost\n\n', 'path': '/workdir/curator/src/bespokelabs/curator/request_processor/online/base_online_request_processor.py'}

Now let me rewrite the body of `handle_single_request_with_retries` to release the reservation on every terminal path. The key changes:
- Success path: `_free_capacity` (settle with used vs blocked) 
- Requeued failure path: `_refund_capacity(blocked_capacity)`
- Exhausted failure path: `_refund_capacity(blocked_capacity)`

str_replace_editor: {'new_str': '        try:\n            # Estimate tokens before making request\n            generic_response = None\n            token_estimate: _TokenUsage = blocked_capacity or self.estimate_total_tokens(request.generic_request.messages)\n\n            # Add new estimate to projection (pre_request=True indicates new estimate)\n            status_tracker.update_cost_projection(token_estimate, pre_request=True)\n\n            generic_response = await self.call_single_request(\n                request=request,\n                session=session,\n                status_tracker=status_tracker,\n            )\n\n            if generic_response.finish_reason in self.config.invalid_finish_reasons:\n                logger.debug(\n                    f"Invalid finish_reason {generic_response.finish_reason}."\n                    " Raw response {generic_response.raw_response} "\n                    "for request {generic_response.raw_request}"\n                )\n                # Update cost projection with actual usage but mark as failed\n                status_tracker.update_stats(generic_response.token_usage, generic_response.response_cost)\n                raise ValueError(f"finish_reason was {generic_response.finish_reason}")\n\n            # Allows us to retry on responses that don\'t match the response format\n            self.prompt_formatter.response_to_response_format(generic_response.response_message)\n\n            # Log cost projection to viewer\n            await self._viewer_client.log_cost_projection(status_tracker)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            status_tracker.num_other_errors += 1\n            request.result.append(e)\n\n            if generic_response and generic_response.token_usage is not None:\n                used_tokens: _TokenUsage = _TokenUsage(input=generic_response.token_usage.input, output=generic_response.token_usage.output)\n                status_tracker.update_cost_projection(used_tokens)\n            else:\n                status_tracker.update_cost_projection(None)\n\n            # The reservation is released on every terminal path through this\n            # branch: both the requeued case (attempts_left > 0) and the\n            # exhausted case (attempts_left == 0) refund the full blocked\n            # estimate, ignoring any reported token usage.\n            self._refund_capacity(status_tracker, blocked_capacity)\n\n            if request.attempts_left > 0:\n                request.attempts_left -= 1\n                logger.warning(\n                    f"Encountered \'{e.__class__.__name__}: {e}\' during attempt "\n                    f"{self.config.max_retries - request.attempts_left} of {self.config.max_retries} "\n                    f"while processing request {request.task_id}"\n                )\n                retry_queue.put_nowait(request)\n            else:\n                error_counts = Counter(str(err) for err in request.result)\n                formatted_errors = [f"{error}(x{count})" for error, count in error_counts.items()]\n                logger.error(\n                    f"Request {request.task_id} failed permanently after exhausting all {self.config.max_retries} retry attempts. "\n                    f"Errors: {formatted_errors}"\n                )\n\n                generic_response = GenericResponse(\n                    response_message=None,\n                    response_errors=formatted_errors,\n                    raw_request=request.api_specific_request,\n                    raw_response=None,\n                    generic_request=request.generic_request,\n                    created_at=request.created_at,\n                    finished_at=datetime.datetime.now(),\n                )\n                await self.append_generic_response(status_tracker, generic_response, response_file)\n                status_tracker.num_tasks_in_progress -= 1\n                status_tracker.num_tasks_failed += 1\n            return\n        else:\n            self._add_output_token_moving_window(generic_response.token_usage.output)\n        finally:\n            if self._semaphore:\n                self._semaphore.release()\n\n        # Save response in the base class\n        await self.append_generic_response(status_tracker, generic_response, response_file)\n\n        status_tracker.num_tasks_in_progress -= 1\n        status_tracker.num_tasks_succeeded += 1\n\n        # Update cost projection with actual usage\n        used_tokens: _TokenUsage = _TokenUsage(input=generic_response.token_usage.input, output=generic_response.token_usage.output)\n        # Update stats with actual usage\n        status_tracker.update_stats(generic_response.token_usage, generic_response.response_cost)\n        status_tracker.update_cost_projection(used_tokens)\n\n        # Free the extra capacity blocked before request with actual consumed capacity.\n        self._free_capacity(status_tracker, used_tokens, blocked_capacity)\n', 'old_str': '        try:\n            # Estimate tokens before making request\n            generic_response = None\n            token_estimate: _TokenUsage = blocked_capacity or self.estimate_total_tokens(request.generic_request.messages)\n\n            # str_replace_editor: {'command': 'str_replace', 'new_str': '    def get_header_based_rate_limits(self) -> tuple[int, _TokenUsage | int]:\n        """Retrieve rate limits from the LLM provider via LiteLLM.\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple[int, _TokenUsage | int]: Contains \'max_requests_per_minute\' and\n                                           \'max_tokens_per_minute\' info.\n\n        Note:\n            - Makes a test request to get rate limit information from response headers.\n            - Some providers (e.g., Claude) require non-empty messages\n        """\n        logger.info(f"Getting rate limits for model: {self.config.model}")\n\n        headers = self.test_call()\n        reading = read_rate_limit_headers(headers)\n        self.apply_rate_limit_reading(reading)\n        return reading.max_requests_per_minute, reading.max_tokens_per_minute', 'old_str': '    def get_header_based_rate_limits(self) -> tuple[int, _TokenUsage | int]:\n        """Retrieve rate limits from the LLM provider via LiteLLM.\n\n        Returns:\n            tuple[int, _TokenUsage | int]: Contains \'max_requests_per_minute\' and\n                                           \'max_tokens_per_minute\' info.\n\n        Note:\n            - Makes a test request to get rate limit information from response headers.\n            - Some providers (e.g., Claude) require non-empty messages\n        """\n        logger.info(f"Getting rate limits for model: {self.config.model}")\n\n        headers = self.test_call()\n        provider = self.config.model.split("/")[0]\n        output_token_key = f"llm_provider-{provider}-ratelimit-output-tokens-remaining"\n        rpm = int(headers.get("x-ratelimit-limit-requests", 0))\n        if output_token_key in headers:\n            self.token_limit_strategy = TokenLimitStrategy.seperate\n            output_tpm = int(headers.get(output_token_key, 0))\n\n            input_token_key = f"llm_provider-{provider}-ratelimit-input-tokens-remaining"\n            input_tpm = int(headers.get(input_token_key, 0))\n            tpm = _TokenUsage(input=input_tpm, output=output_tpm)\n\n        else:\n            tpm = int(headers.get("x-ratelimit-limit-tokens", 0))\n\n        return rpm, tpm', 'path': '/workdir/curator/src/bespokelabs/curator/request_processor/online/litellm_online_request_processor.py'}

For litellm with `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6`, `_set_manual_tpm` is called: since strategy is combined initially, `manual_max_tokens_per_minute = config.max_tokens_per_minute` = None. Let me summarize what I've implemented:

## Summary

I implemented the capacity-budget module refactor as specified in the ticket:

### New module `src/bespokelabs/curator/status_tracker/capacity_budget.py`
- All required constants (`DEFAULT_*`, `LIMIT_ORIGIN_*`, `CAPACITY_DEBT_FLOOR_FRACTION`, header-name tuples)
- `TokenLimitStrategy` enum (moved here, re-exported via `online_status_tracker`)
- `CapacityExceedsLimitError(ValueError)` with `.axis/.requested/.limit` and the exact `__str__`
- `@dataclass(frozen=True) RateLimitReading` with the correct field order
- `parse_limit_value` (bool rejected, int/str decoding with kKmM suffixes, everything else â None)
- `read_rate_limit_headers` (lowercases once, consults only the 4 name tuples, strategy selection in reading, no remaining/reset/llm_provider reads, no numeric fallbacks)

### `OnlineStatusTracker` changes
- Removed duplicate `max_tokens_per_minute` field; new field types (`float | None`, `int | _TokenUsage | None = 0`, etc.)
- Added `capacity_clock` injectable callable, `token_limit_origin`/`request_limit_origin`, and the three counters (`num_capacity_debt_clamps`, `num_capacity_settlements`, `num_capacity_refunds`)
- `__post_init__` â `_normalise_limits` doing shape coercion, origin resolution (unlimited/defaulted/configured axis-by-axis), and seeding both buckets to full limit; re-seeds `last_update_time` via the clock
- `update_capacity` uses the clock, clamps elapsed â¥ 0, caps refill at limit, assigns `last_update_time` unconditionally, `math.floor` refill under separate
- `has_capacity` runs feasibility check (raising `CapacityExceedsLimitError`) **before** `update_capacity`; unlimited admits any estimate; empty bucket returns False
- `consume_capacity` / `free_capacity` (settle by `blocked - used`) / `refund_capacity` (refund full blocked + 1 request slot, capped) with per-axis debt flooring at `-limit * 0.25`; separate mutations rebind fresh `_TokenUsage` so `.total` is never stale

### `BaseOnlineRequestProcessor` changes
- `_reserve_capacity` (estimate once unconditionally, has_capacity, consume on success)
- `_free_capacity` / `_refund_capacity` delegates
- `apply_rate_limit_reading` (only writer of processor state from a reading, re-reads default for new strategy)
- Both reservation loops now `while (token_estimate := self._reserve_capacity(...)) is None: await asyncio.sleep(0.1)`
- `handle_single_request_with_retries` releases the reservation on every terminal path (success â settle, both failure paths â refund)
- Deleted the dead `free_capacity` method

### Provider processors
- anthropic & litellm `get_header_based_rate_limits` delegate to `read_rate_limit_headers` + `apply_rate_limit_reading`, dropping swapped mapping, fallbacks, `-remaining` reads, and in-place strategy mutation
- openai delegates only the header-half fallback; the `RATE_LIMIT_HEADER` provider table and rps/tps scaling stay unchanged

### Tests
- New `tests/unittests/test_capacity_budget.py` with 46 tests covering parsing, header reading, normalisation, refill/clock, capacity operations (r1/r2 worked cases), error semantics, processor helpers, and one async coroutine (no real `asyncio.sleep` waits) â all pass alongside the existing relevant test suites.
